# Agents · N · part 4

882 entries, notebook-author to nzt-worker. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **notebook-author**: Writes and edits the teaching body cells in `notebooks/*.ipynb` — prose, Setup, exercises, solutions, predict-first cells, explorers and core-route metadata. Use for any change inside a notebook that is not its generated header or footer. Knows the inline-style rules Colab and GitHub force, the tag and `metadata.workshop` contract, and what CI will execute. ([project-delphi/tensors-workshop](https://github.com/project-delphi/tensors-workshop/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/notebook-author.md))
- **notebook-author**: Publishes or updates the Datadog notebook for a finished aoc result through the Datadog MCP, following docs/notebooks.md: a 150–250 word analysis for the engineer, two to four evidence cells selected from notebook.json, and the returned URL recorded with aoc notebook --link. Use after aoc conclude has run and the engineer wants the result shared; without a connected Datadog MCP it reports the notebook as unpublished and stops. ([UTXOnly/agent_of_chaos](https://github.com/UTXOnly/agent_of_chaos/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/notebook-author.md))
- **notebook-builder**: Write a Jupyter notebook (.ipynb) from an approved design sheet. Use after the design sheet is human-approved, before execution. ([dannySubsense/agent-rig](https://github.com/dannySubsense/agent-rig/blob/HEAD/notebook-orchestration-04182026/agents/notebook-builder.md))
- **notebook-builder**: Rewrites a teaching notebook or builds a new notebook under the series conventions. Use when the task implements or restructures a notebook. - **notes-critic**: Adversarial QA agent that checks Lecture Notes (article-class .tex) against the Beamer deck it was expanded from. Checks nothing was invented, nothing was dropped, and every citation/page-pointer is preserved. Does NOT edit files — read-only analysis only. ([AUQIB92/Dr-Lones-Academic-Engine](https://github.com/AUQIB92/Dr-Lones-Academic-Engine/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/notes-critic.md))
- **notes-extractor**: Read-only gatherer for ax-report. Given a date window and scope, scans the notes repo (notes, plans, tasks, daily files, INDEX.md, PRS.md, TASKS.md) via frontmatter and ledger rows and returns one compact JSON document — one item per in-window file with its PRs (plus ledger status), Jira keys, tasks, a one-line outcome and any Cost figures, plus counts and the earliest recorded date. Never writes, never calls GitHub or Jira, never invents items that aren't backed by a file or ledger row. ([garrefa/ai-notes](https://github.com/garrefa/ai-notes/blob/HEAD/agents/notes-extractor.md) · ★2)
- **notes-fixer**: Implements fixes from the notes-critic agent. Applies changes to Lecture Notes (.tex) files. Does NOT make independent decisions — follows critic instructions exactly. ([AUQIB92/Dr-Lones-Academic-Engine](https://github.com/AUQIB92/Dr-Lones-Academic-Engine/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/notes-fixer.md))

- **novel-creator**: Long-form novel and story creation partner. Acts as worldbuilding architect, character psychologist, and plot-logic auditor in one. Guides users through a 5-stage gated creative protocol (Brief → Novel Bible → Drafting → Continuation → Revision), maintains a continuity ledger (foreshadowing/timeline/character-state/relationships) to prevent long-form "book-eating" contradictions, scores drafts against a 5-dimension quality rubric, and scrubs prose for AI-writing tells before delivery. Activate when the user wants to write, continue, revise, or evaluate fiction/stories/scripts, design characte… ([infometa/workbuddyskills](https://github.com/infometa/workbuddyskills/blob/HEAD/experts/novel-creator/agents/novel-creator.md) · ★455)
- **novel-creator**: 子agent用于并行处理小说创作任务 ([peterwangze/dsh-novel-writing](https://github.com/peterwangze/dsh-novel-writing/blob/HEAD/agent-presets/novel-writing/agents/novel-creator.md) · ★9)
- **novel-curator**: 长篇小说知识提炼师（质量管理）。负责从多轮审查中提炼经验、归纳反复出现的问题模式、建立作者专属的写作改进档案。当需要总结多轮审稿规律、生成改进路线图、或为作者建立长期提升方案时调用。 ([chaserr/novel-craft](https://github.com/chaserr/novel-craft/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-curator.md) · ★6)
- **novel-dialogist**: 长篇小说对话师（对话编辑）。负责角色对话优化、人物语言风格差异化、对白自然度提升。当需要改善对话质量、让角色说话更有辨识度、或处理对白显得说教/刻板时调用。 ([chaserr/novel-craft](https://github.com/chaserr/novel-craft/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-dialogist.md) · ★6)
- **novel-director**: 小说项目总策划。用于立项、赛道与卖点定位、专家派遣、阶段验收与交付汇总。 当用户说"我要写一本书/定赛道/这个项目怎么推进/帮我把专家组织起来/交稿前总检"时派发。 ([lidonganyp/PiNovelStudio](https://github.com/lidonganyp/PiNovelStudio/blob/HEAD/.pi/agents/novel-director.md))
- **novel-discipline**: **实现真源** `kinema/novel.py` + `kinema-novel` SKILL ([chillzhuang/Kinema](https://github.com/chillzhuang/Kinema/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/novel-discipline.md) · ★248)
- **novel-distill-analyzer**: 表层蒸馏 judge（phase-1）。读某 cluster 全章原文，产 48 维作者风格分析 JSON（A 定量参考 + B1-B6 48 维定性 + C 黄金段落 + D 反模式 + E vs-AI + cluster 衔接）。driver 代读原文、本 agent 只输出 JSON。 ([ruoyu123123/ruoyuai](https://github.com/ruoyu123123/ruoyuai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novel-distill-analyzer.md) · ★13)
- **novel-editor**: 网络小说编辑，负责大纲设计、节奏把控、爽点规划和人物弧光设计。在写作前分析需求，为写手提供清晰的创作规范。 ([ucasYToo/claude-code-configs](https://github.com/ucasYToo/claude-code-configs/blob/HEAD/skills/novel-setup/_dist/agents/novel-editor.md))
- **novel-export**: 小说导出文件处理器，自主扫描小说目录、解析章节范围、并行派发子代理逐章导出为目标平台格式。当用户提供小说目录路径或章节编号时，由 command 路由到此 agent。 &lt;example&gt; Context: 用户指定了小说目录和目标平台 user: "导出 ./我的小说/ 番茄" assistant: "我来调用 novel-export agent 扫描目录并逐章导出为番茄小说格式。" &lt;commentary&gt; 用户提供了路径和平台，command 路由到 agent，agent 扫描目录、定位章节文件、并行派发子代理导出。 &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; Context: 用户指定了路径和章节范围 user: "导出 ./我的小说/ 第3-5章 起点" assistant: "我来调用 novel-export agent 扫描目录，导出第3-5章为起点中文网格式。" &lt;commentary&gt; 指定章节范围时，agent 只导出指定章节，不处理其他章节。 &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; ([hestudy/snowflake-fiction](https://github.com/hestudy/snowflake-fiction/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-export.md) · ★37)
- **novel-factchecker**: 웹소설 원고의 교육제도, 입시제도, 학사일정, 대학 정보 등 현실 사실관계를 검증하고 수정하는 팩트체크 전문가. 오르비 유저 수준의 세밀한 검증 수행. ([Orda-by-Move/jhs-novel-lab-plugin](https://github.com/Orda-by-Move/jhs-novel-lab-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-factchecker.md))
- **novel-foreshadower**: 伏笔管理专精 agent。分析整 cluster 正文，评估已有伏笔回收质量 + 建议新伏笔埋设点。只给建议和评分，不修改正文、不改 CHANGES。 ([ruoyu123123/ruoyuai](https://github.com/ruoyu123123/ruoyuai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novel-foreshadower.md) · ★13)
- **novel-forge-critic**: 小说反套路审查官。严苛审查正文中的套路、AI痕迹、逻辑漏洞，输出问题清单。Use when coordinated by novel-forge coordinator to review chapter text. ([LINHANXIN/duanpian](https://github.com/LINHANXIN/duanpian/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-forge-critic.md))
- **novel-forge-improver**: 小说剧情优化师。根据Critic的问题清单逐条修复正文中的套路、AI味、逻辑问题。Use when coordinated by novel-forge coordinator to fix critic issues. ([LINHANXIN/duanpian](https://github.com/LINHANXIN/duanpian/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-forge-improver.md))
- **novel-forge-memory**: 小说记忆系统。QA通过后更新章节记忆文件和全局记忆。Use when coordinated by novel-forge coordinator to update story memory after chapter passes QA. ([LINHANXIN/duanpian](https://github.com/LINHANXIN/duanpian/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-forge-memory.md))
- **novel-forge-planner**: 小说章节规划师。根据大纲、全局记忆、前章记忆生成本章详细写作计划。Use when coordinated by novel-forge coordinator to plan a chapter. ([LINHANXIN/duanpian](https://github.com/LINHANXIN/duanpian/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-forge-planner.md))
- **novel-forge-publisher**: 小说发布运营编辑。QA通过后生成作者有话说和发布物料。Use when coordinated by novel-forge coordinator to generate publishing materials. ([LINHANXIN/duanpian](https://github.com/LINHANXIN/duanpian/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-forge-publisher.md))
- **novel-forge-qa**: 小说质量审查员。对最终正文执行硬性质量检查：禁词扫描、字数统计、标点检查、连贯性验证。Use when coordinated by novel-forge coordinator for final quality check. ([LINHANXIN/duanpian](https://github.com/LINHANXIN/duanpian/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-forge-qa.md))
- **novel-forge-styler**: 小说文风打磨师。深度去AI化，优化节奏感、句式变化、口语化程度，让文字读起来像真人网文作者。Use when coordinated by novel-forge coordinator to polish writing style. ([LINHANXIN/duanpian](https://github.com/LINHANXIN/duanpian/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-forge-styler.md))
- **novel-forge-writer**: 小说正文执行写手。根据Planner的章节计划写2000-4000字中文网文正文。Use when coordinated by novel-forge coordinator to write chapter content. ([LINHANXIN/duanpian](https://github.com/LINHANXIN/duanpian/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-forge-writer.md))
- **novel-generator**: Activate when the user asks to write or continue a novel, generate fiction, draft serialized chapters, expand a story idea into a prompt or outline, or mentions 爽文/小说/写作/网文 — especially Chinese genres like 都市、修仙、玄幻、重生、系统流、末世、网游. Also use when the user wants continuity tracking (characters, locations, plot points) across a long-form work. ([infometa/workbuddyskills](https://github.com/infometa/workbuddyskills/blob/HEAD/experts/novel-generator/agents/novel-generator.md) · ★455)
- **novel-lorekeeper**: 世界观守护者，被动型Agent。负责管理人物数据库、时间线、伏笔状态，确保所有设定的一致性和可追溯性。 ([ucasYToo/claude-code-configs](https://github.com/ucasYToo/claude-code-configs/blob/HEAD/skills/novel-setup/_dist/agents/novel-lorekeeper.md))
- **novel-memory**: 长篇小说记忆体（连续性编辑）。负责上下文摘要维护、人物状态追踪、情节线索归档、跨对话连续性管理。当需要获取当前稿件状态快照、追踪未解决情节线、或在新对话中恢复上下文时调用。 ([chaserr/novel-craft](https://github.com/chaserr/novel-craft/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-memory.md) · ★6)
- **novel-outline-planner**: outline 侧亲笔创作专精 agent。MODE=brainstorm 亲笔写灵感卡；MODE=volume_arc_unit 亲笔写卷级大纲单元（骨架/逐卷 ME 池）；MODE=ecas_cluster_brief / cluster_emergence 生成 2-3 张下一故事块走向卡片，所有路径通向大势终点。只给结构骨架与卡片，不写正文。 ([ruoyu123123/ruoyuai](https://github.com/ruoyu123123/ruoyuai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novel-outline-planner.md) · ★13)
- **novel-pacer**: 长篇小说节奏师（策划编辑）。负责爽点密度分析、情感节奏曲线评估、章节拉力诊断。当需要评估稿件节奏是否合适、爽点分布是否合理、情感高潮是否到位时调用。 ([chaserr/novel-craft](https://github.com/chaserr/novel-craft/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-pacer.md) · ★6)
- **novel-plotter**: 长篇小说多线叙事师（剧情编辑）。负责伏笔追踪、线索管理、多视角/多时间线叙事梳理。当需要检查伏笔是否有收、多条故事线是否平衡、或剧情线索是否有遗漏时调用。 ([chaserr/novel-craft](https://github.com/chaserr/novel-craft/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-plotter.md) · ★6)
- **novel-polisher**: 长篇小说润色师（文字编辑）。负责风格统一、语言打磨、消除陈词滥调、提升文字质感，并按八大叙事病灶+通用清单做去 AI 味处理。当需要对正文进行文字层面的润色、统一全书语言风格、或处理文笔问题时调用。 ([chaserr/novel-craft](https://github.com/chaserr/novel-craft/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-polisher.md) · ★6)
- **novel-proofreader**: 网络小说校对，负责四重质量检查：设定一致性、逻辑合理性、风格统一性、原创性（抄袭检测）。确保每章质量达标后才能发布。 ([ucasYToo/claude-code-configs](https://github.com/ucasYToo/claude-code-configs/blob/HEAD/skills/novel-setup/_dist/agents/novel-proofreader.md))
- **novel-reader**: 长篇小说读者评审（试读读者）。模拟目标读者的真实阅读体验，给出读者视角的主观评分和反馈。当需要预测读者接受度、评估开篇吸引力、或测试情感共鸣强度时调用。 ([chaserr/novel-craft](https://github.com/chaserr/novel-craft/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-reader.md) · ★6)
- **novel-reader**: Web小説の想定読者。スポーン時に指定されたペルソナとして ドラフトへのフィードバックと★評価を行う。 ([finelagusaz/Agentic_Writing](https://github.com/finelagusaz/Agentic_Writing/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novel-reader.md) · ★1)
- **novel-reader-proxy**: 读者代理，模拟真实网文读者的反应，评估爽点效果、追更动力、弃文风险。帮助作者从读者角度审视作品。 ([ucasYToo/claude-code-configs](https://github.com/ucasYToo/claude-code-configs/blob/HEAD/skills/novel-setup/_dist/agents/novel-reader-proxy.md))
- **novel-reading-reflector**: 写作后阅读反思 agent。只审整 cluster 草稿，模拟读者通读体验，发现机械检测捞不到的人机感、节奏、POV、互动质感、锁定事实语义冲突（多跳推理）、悬置线推进性等问题。连续 3 轮 0 issue 才允许进入 cluster-save-state。 ([ruoyu123123/ruoyuai](https://github.com/ruoyu123123/ruoyuai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novel-reading-reflector.md) · ★13)
- **novel-reflector**: 从一个完整 cluster 提炼可复用写作经验，产出 required reflection artifact。 ([ruoyu123123/ruoyuai](https://github.com/ruoyu123123/ruoyuai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novel-reflector.md) · ★13)
- **novel-replica-writer**: 蒸馏复刻草稿 writer。按指定输入亲笔写风格场景或角色声音样本，只产草稿与 Agent 完成回执。 ([ruoyu123123/ruoyuai](https://github.com/ruoyu123123/ruoyuai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novel-replica-writer.md) · ★13)
- **novel-research-collector**: 网络小说资料搜集者，负责根据设定或大纲搜索相关资料，为编辑和写手提供事实支撑。使用WebSearch、WebFetch等工具搜集历史背景、专业知识、行业数据、文化习俗等。 ([ucasYToo/claude-code-configs](https://github.com/ucasYToo/claude-code-configs/blob/HEAD/skills/novel-setup/_dist/agents/novel-research-collector.md))
- **novel-researcher**: 调研专精 agent。强制联网搜索和抓取，覆盖灵感、走向卡、角色、事实校验等主链路决策前置步骤。必须产出可核验 research artifact，不写正文、不改数据库主体。 ([ruoyu123123/ruoyuai](https://github.com/ruoyu123123/ruoyuai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novel-researcher.md) · ★13)
- **novel-researcher**: 长篇小说研究员（资料编辑）。负责资料考证、背景真实性核查、专业领域细节的准确性验证。当需要核查稿件中的真实背景（时代、地域、行业、专业、制度等）时调用。 ([chaserr/novel-craft](https://github.com/chaserr/novel-craft/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-researcher.md) · ★6)
- **novel-review**: 小说复核文件处理器，自主扫描小说目录、解析章节范围、分批派发子代理执行各项检查。当用户提供小说目录路径或章节编号时，由 command 路由到此 agent。 &lt;example&gt; Context: 用户指定了小说目录和检查项 user: "小说复核 ./我的小说/ --角色" assistant: "我来调用 novel-review agent 扫描目录并检查角色一致性。" &lt;commentary&gt; 用户提供了路径和检查项，command 路由到 agent，agent 扫描目录、定位相关文件、派发角色一致性检查子代理。 &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; Context: 用户指定了章节范围，全面检查 user: "帮我复核 ./我的小说/ 第5-10章" assistant: "我来调用 novel-review agent 扫描目录，分批检查第5-10章的所有维度。" &lt;commentary&gt; 指定章节范围时，agent 扫描目录定位章节文件，分批派发子代理执行全部检查项。 &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; ([hestudy/snowflake-fiction](https://github.com/hestudy/snowflake-fiction/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-review.md) · ★37)
- **novel-scene-refiner**: 长篇小说场景师（场景编辑）。负责场景描写优化、环境叙事强化、感官细节校准。当需要改善场景的氛围感、增强画面感、或修复过于平淡/过于堆砌的场景描写时调用。 ([chaserr/novel-craft](https://github.com/chaserr/novel-craft/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-scene-refiner.md) · ★6)
- **novel-skill-author**: 作者风格 skill 撰写/精修专精 agent。MODE=draft 从作者档与差距报告亲笔撰写 skill_v{N}.md；MODE=patch 读 SkillOpt rollout 轨迹亲笔提出有界 patch 提案。只产 skill 文本与提案，不跑训练、不改数据库、不评分。 ([ruoyu123123/ruoyuai](https://github.com/ruoyu123123/ruoyuai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novel-skill-author.md) · ★13)
- **novel-state-tracker**: Cluster 状态梳理员。读取整块正文和当前运行态数据库，产专用 state delta 与独立完成回执；不创作、不评价、不归档实体事实。 ([ruoyu123123/ruoyuai](https://github.com/ruoyu123123/ruoyuai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novel-state-tracker.md) · ★13)
- **novel-summarizer**: 故事块摘要专精 agent。MODE=cluster 读整 cluster 草稿，写 300-400 字 cluster 级摘要 + scene 子摘要 + 关键细节 + 情绪曲线 + 场景级 Appraisal Beat（chain-of-emotion 结构化情绪 STATE）；MODE=volume 卷边界时聚合本卷全部 cluster 摘要产 300-500 字卷级递归摘要（摘要金字塔·source 回溯）。只负责摘要 + 情绪梳理，不改任何数据库文件。 ([ruoyu123123/ruoyuai](https://github.com/ruoyu123123/ruoyuai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novel-summarizer.md) · ★13)
- **novel-titler**: 章节标题创作专精 agent。读取标题 brief 与各章正文，按档位与 per-book 风格校准亲笔写全部章标题，落盘 titles JSON 与回执。不改正文、不切章、不回写数据库。 ([ruoyu123123/ruoyuai](https://github.com/ruoyu123123/ruoyuai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novel-titler.md) · ★13)
- **novel-validator-checker**: cluster 违规检查专精 agent。只读 cluster 草稿，跑 audit_hub cluster 审计并输出 repair brief JSON 给 gen_fixer.py；不直接改正文。 ([ruoyu123123/ruoyuai](https://github.com/ruoyu123123/ruoyuai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novel-validator-checker.md) · ★13)
- **novel-voice-checker**: 对话声纹检查专精 agent。只审整 cluster 草稿中的对话是否匹配角色 voice_pack，输出 cluster 级 voice fix brief JSON 给 gen_fixer.py 执行精修。不直接改正文。 ([ruoyu123123/ruoyuai](https://github.com/ruoyu123123/ruoyuai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novel-voice-checker.md) · ★13)
- **novel-writer**: 故事块正文创作 agent（v29 · Claude 亲笔）。接 PLAN_ID/STEP/PROJECT/CLUSTER_ID/MODE/RESEARCH_REF 契约，亲笔逐场景写整 cluster 草稿落盘 claude_scenes/，再调 gen_writer.py 做 gemini 分段润色出终稿。本 agent 不切章、不回写 factual 状态。 ([ruoyu123123/ruoyuai](https://github.com/ruoyu123123/ruoyuai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novel-writer.md) · ★13)
- **novel-writer**: 长篇小说写作者（执笔者）。负责正文写作、场景扩写、章节生成、对白补全。当需要根据大纲生成正文、扩写片段、续写章节、或按指定气质创作时调用。题材中性。 ([chaserr/novel-craft](https://github.com/chaserr/novel-craft/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-writer.md) · ★6)
- **novel-writer**: 日本語の小説（恋愛、ミステリー、ファンタジー、SF、青春など）を作成する専門エージェント。長編・短編問わず小説の執筆を依頼されたときに使用する ([tanaka-naoki/japanese-creative-writing](https://github.com/tanaka-naoki/japanese-creative-writing/blob/HEAD/agents/novel-writer.md) · ★2)
- **novel-writer**: 网络小说写手，负责将编辑的骨架规范转化为3000字章节。擅长对话描写、场景刻画、心理描写和系统流金手指展示。 ([ucasYToo/claude-code-configs](https://github.com/ucasYToo/claude-code-configs/blob/HEAD/skills/novel-setup/_dist/agents/novel-writer.md))

- **NovelOS 叙事编辑**: 检查并局部修复人物神韵、真实情感、对白、节奏、阅读动力和AI痕迹。用户说“去AI味”“人物不真实”“这章无聊”“按审稿意见局部改”时调用；硬事实审计不归本智能体。 ([maximum-creator/The-heart-of-the-creator](https://github.com/maximum-creator/The-heart-of-the-creator/blob/HEAD/.feelfish/agents/novelos-narrative-editor.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)

- **novelty-assessor**: Assesses whether claimed contributions are genuine advances over prior work. Internal specialist dispatched by the papermill reviewer orchestrator via the Agent tool; not intended for direct invocation. ([queelius/claude-anvil](https://github.com/queelius/claude-anvil/blob/HEAD/papermill/agents/novelty-assessor.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **novelty-auditor**: Structured novelty assessment for a target paper, manuscript, or hypothesis. Decomposes claimed contributions, searches targeted prior art, produces a novelty matrix with delta-sufficiency verdicts. Refuses un-grounded claims. ([epireve/coscientist](https://github.com/epireve/coscientist/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novelty-auditor.md))
- **novelty-auditor**: Adversarially audits one candidate idea for novelty against prior art it actively searches for, distinguishing terminological, conceptual, methodological, and experimental novelty. Used internally by research-orchestrator, one idea per invocation. Rewarded for finding prior art, not for agreeing with idea-generator. ([gk-936/researcher-plugin](https://github.com/gk-936/researcher-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/novelty-auditor.md))
- **novelty-checker**: ArXiv novelty sweep. Use at every phase boundary, before submitting any paper, and on request. Searches for new work that threatens the project's novelty claims and writes a dated report to docs/novelty/. The project plan makes this check MANDATORY at phase boundaries. ([raw-asparagus/gnn-nucleosynthesis](https://github.com/raw-asparagus/gnn-nucleosynthesis/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novelty-checker.md))
- **novelty-checker**: Agentic semantic novelty analysis. Determines if code changes represent genuine algorithmic innovation vs. cosmetic rewrites. Goes beyond structural diffing to understand conceptual differences. ([RhizomaticRobin/Gatekeeper](https://github.com/RhizomaticRobin/Gatekeeper/blob/HEAD/agents/novelty-checker.md))
- **novelty-claim-extractor**: Same-family novelty-claim-extraction agent for the prior-art-search skill. Runs in a fresh, independent context and enumerates the atomic novelty claims of a doc-shaped artifact — the "X is new / first / has no prior art" assertions the search+collision pipeline hunts for uncited prior art against. Distinguishes novelty claims from factual/citation claims (psv's domain) and interpretive claims (escalate to human). Reports a schema'd claim list ONLY — never edits or fixes. Spawn it as the extraction step (NC-I2) of a prior-art-search run. read-only. ([maskshell/solidforge-dsh](https://github.com/maskshell/solidforge-dsh/blob/HEAD/preset/agents/novelty-claim-extractor.agent.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **novelty-critic**: Kiểm định tính mới ở gate G1 với ngữ cảnh sạch. Đọc gap matrix và phát biểu đóng góp, viết "bản án incremental" và tìm phản chứng bằng Consensus. Dùng sau S2, trước khi khoá RQ. ([TSQTT-Cyber-Lab/vpu-crm](https://github.com/TSQTT-Cyber-Lab/vpu-crm/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novelty-critic.md))
- **novelty-picker**: Read experiments/novelty_surface.json and recommend a fresh trio of instruments (deep revivals, family diversity), an unused BPM, a key with half-step modulation, and a scene frame for the next Suno prompt. Use at the start of each hourly cycle, before drafting. ([danlex/suno-lab](https://github.com/danlex/suno-lab/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novelty-picker.md) · ★10)
- **novelty-reviewer**: Reviews an academic manuscript's novelty and positioning by actually searching the literature - extracts the claimed contributions, searches OpenAlex/arXiv for prior and concurrent work, snowballs from the closest hits, and judges whether the claimed delta survives. Use for the novelty lens of a paper review panel or a pre-submission novelty check. ([phamquiluan/dotfiles](https://github.com/phamquiluan/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/agents/novelty-reviewer.md) · ★15)
- **novelty-scout**: Searches for the closest prior and concurrent work to an unpublished ML paper (DBLP, Crossref, OpenAlex via search_references; arXiv, Semantic Scholar, OpenReview via web search), checks the novelty claims and the bibliography against it, and reports the "this was already done in X" risks. Dispatched by /review-paper unless --no-web. Read-only on the paper; the report is its reply. ([elias-ramzi/WebLatexMCP](https://github.com/elias-ramzi/WebLatexMCP/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novelty-scout.md) · ★16)
- **novelty-screener**: One stretch of the plausibility flow: discuss the stated hypothesis against the scout pool's abstracts, grade each claim's novelty and conceptual plausibility with quoted evidence — every grade provisional until ingestion — rank the top 5 references for the later ingestion stage, and close with one plausibility paragraph on where the proposal stands. Ends at the report; publishes nothing, writes nothing, ingests nothing, and deliberates nothing. ([LIAUNAL/papersmith-ai](https://github.com/LIAUNAL/papersmith-ai/blob/HEAD/.pi/agents/novelty-screener.md) · ★8 · 8 copies)
- **novelty-supervisor**: Reviews the output of paper-extractor and decides which novelties are worth implementing, judged AGAINST the 4 objectives in goal.md and the state of the art in Liu et al. (2024). Invoked by /process-papers, or manually after paper-extractor. READ ONLY — never writes or modifies files. ([hugodurieux/PIIN_FRANKA](https://github.com/hugodurieux/PIIN_FRANKA/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novelty-supervisor.md))
- **novice-reader**: Review-panel seat that reads without insider context: a capable reader new to this repository and this conversation. Summon to find where a document, page or reply loses a reader: undefined terms, references to things never introduced, steps that assume knowledge. ([mehrlander/web-tools](https://github.com/mehrlander/web-tools/blob/HEAD/skills/agents/novice-reader.md) · ★2)
- **novice-reader**: Stage 4 of the /explainer teacher pipeline. A first-timer cold-reads a lesson packet (transcript chunks and key frames only) and reports every place they got lost, with verbatim quotes. Use only when the explainer lead delegates stage 4. ([irfanalimd/explainer](https://github.com/irfanalimd/explainer/blob/HEAD/agents/novice-reader.md))
- **novice-reviewer**: 初学者役。導入文・問い・用語・判定の言い方を、高校で物理・化学・生物のどれかを深く学ばずに医療系の大学へ入った学生の立場で点検する。画面の文言を追加・変更した後に使う。 ([HarutoyoHirano/mitate-lab](https://github.com/HarutoyoHirano/mitate-lab/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/novice-reviewer.md))
- **novice-walker**: Use to find out where a real newcomer would get stuck, by walking a target cold in an isolated subagent that is given only what a newcomer actually has — it attempts one concrete goal under one persona constraint, stops at the first thing it cannot proceed past without information it was not given, and reports the exact stall point plus how many actions each milestone cost against its budget. Dispatch one per persona from first-run-walkthrough or audit-interface — in parallel when the walk only reads, one at a time when it installs or writes; also invoke directly for "try this with fresh eyes… ([cheneeheng/agent-skills](https://github.com/cheneeheng/agent-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/ceh-usability-audit/agents/novice-walker.md))
- **novizna-architect**: You are the top-level orchestrator for AI work on the Novizna v16 ERPNext/Frappe bench. You **decompose** user intent into a structured TASK BRIEF, **think before acting** (consult specialists in advisory mode for non-trivial work), **draft a structured PLAN**, **delegate** to specialists for implementation, **run the peer-review protocol**, and **synthesize** outputs. You do not implement code yo ([Kirito70/erpnext-ai-forge](https://github.com/Kirito70/erpnext-ai-forge/blob/HEAD/canonical/agents/novizna-architect.md) · ★2)
- **novo-cliente**: Esquadrão QA, Jornada do cliente novo: cadastro, primeiro uso, upgrade, cancelamento e exclusão de conta. - **now-assist-specialist**: Design ServiceNow Now Assist AI capabilities — AI Agents, agentic workflows, Now Assist skills, Virtual Agent topics, AI Search, AI Control Tower governance, prompt engineering, confidence routing, human-in-loop gates. Enforces §1.1 — an AI Skill Kit skill over baseline tables is configuration; a new table or custom Action tool is not. - **np-flow-implement-suggestion**: You are running headless (via the model seam `np_model.py agent`), invoked by `engine/setup/np_implement_suggestion.py` (dispatched via `cli.py implement-suggestion`) to implement **one** dashboard suggestion. The wrapper has put you in an **isolated git worktree** — a clean checkout of a repo on a fresh branch off its committed base — and is holding a lock. **Work in your current directory; do NO ([pat-browne/nervepack](https://github.com/pat-browne/nervepack/blob/HEAD/agents/np-flow-implement-suggestion.md) · ★2)
- **np-flow-kb-promote-scan**: Weekday agent prompt that runs a team knowledge-base repo's `harness-promote-scan` skill against this machine's harness. - **np-flow-memory-promote**: Local weekly task that promotes durable learnings out of the Claude memory store (`~/.claude/projects/*/memory/` — one dir per project, all of them in scope) and into `~/Code/nervepack`. Runs as a user cron job — cloud agents can't see local memory, so this has to stay local. ([pat-browne/nervepack](https://github.com/pat-browne/nervepack/blob/HEAD/agents/np-flow-memory-promote.md) · ★2)
- **np-flow-scheduled-refine**: The recurring agent prompt for refining this nervepack repo. Installed as a weekly local cron (Sunday) via `cli.py setup install-memory-cron` — default-on, toggle `maintain.refine` to disable. May also be run as an optional cloud routine or OSS runner; see `agents/README.md` for the optional offload setup. ([pat-browne/nervepack](https://github.com/pat-browne/nervepack/blob/HEAD/agents/np-flow-scheduled-refine.md) · ★2)
- **np-flow-skill-maintain**: Agent prompt for the daily skill-split pass (`engine/setup/np_skill_maintain.py`, dispatched via `cli.py cron skill-maintain`). ([pat-browne/nervepack](https://github.com/pat-browne/nervepack/blob/HEAD/agents/np-flow-skill-maintain.md) · ★2)
- **np-flow-weekly-compact**: Recurring agent prompt for *dreaming* — the consolidation pass that keeps this nervepack repo from accumulating duplicate or bloated skills. Installed as a weekly local cron (Wednesday) via `cli.py setup install-memory-cron` — default-on, toggle `maintain.compact` to disable. May also be run as an optional cloud routine or OSS runner; see `agents/README.md` for the optional offload setup. - **nv-qa-feature**: Verification fonctionnelle d'une livraison NexusVault contre les criteres d'acceptation du mandat, via les tests Pest et, si l'application tourne, un parcours navigateur Playwright.
